  String _weekday(int d)=>const ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'][d-1];
  String _month(int m)=>const ['January','February','March','April','May','June',
    'July','August','September','October','November','December'][m-1];
  String _financialYear(DateTime d){
    final y=d.month>=4?d.year:d.year-1;
    return '01-04-$y  –  31-03-${y+1}';
  }
  Widget _heroAction(IconData icon,String label,VoidCallback onPressed)=>
    Material(color:Colors.white,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(30),child:InkWell(
      onTap:onPressed,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(30),child:Tooltip(message:label,
      child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(10),child:Icon(icon,size:18,color:teal)))));
  Widget _premiumSurface({required Widget child,EdgeInsetsGeometry padding=const EdgeInsets.all(12)})=>Container(
    padding:padding,decoration:BoxDecoration(color:Colors.white,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(20),
      border:Border.all(color:const Color(0xFFE1EAEC)),boxShadow:const [BoxShadow(
        color:Color(0x160F5258),blurRadius:18,offset:Offset(0,7))]),child:child);
  // Report-viewer style navigation: clean cards with clear icons and short titles.
  Widget _premiumModuleTile(String name,IconData icon,Color a,Color b,VoidCallback action)=>
    Material(color:Colors.transparent,child:InkWell(onTap:action,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(18),
      child:Ink(decoration:BoxDecoration(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(18),
        color:Colors.white,border:Border.all(color:const Color(0xFFD3E5E4)),
        boxShadow:const [BoxShadow(color:Color(0x130A5359),blurRadius:10,offset:Offset(0,4))]),
        child:Column(mainAxisAlignment:MainAxisAlignment.center,children:[
          Container(width:48,height:48,decoration:BoxDecoration(
            color:a,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(14)),
            child:Icon(icon,size:29,color:const Color(0xFF075D61))),
          const SizedBox(height:9),
          Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:7),child:Text(name,
            maxLines:2,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis,textAlign:TextAlign.center,
            style:const TextStyle(fontSize:14,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink,height:1.12))),
        ]))));

  Widget _listing(Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    if(tab=='Production Reports' || tab=='Chokar Stock' || tab=='Chokar Customer Sales') {
      return Column(children:[
        if(tab!='Chokar Stock') _filters(),
        Expanded(child:ChokarMobileReports(
          snapshot:data,
          mode:tab=='Production Reports'?'production':tab=='Chokar Stock'?'stock':'customers',
          query:query, fromDate:tab=='Chokar Stock'?null:fromDate,
          toDate:tab=='Chokar Stock'?null:toDate,
        )),
      ]);
    }
    if(tab=='Settings') return _settingsPage();
    if(tab=='Connection') return _connection();
    if(tab=='Security & Login') return _securityPage();
    if(tab=='Ledger') return _accounts(data,forLedger:true);
    if(tab=='Accounts') return _accounts(data);
    if(tab=='Account Ledger') return _ledger(data);
    if(tab=='Payment & Receipt') return _receipts(data);
    if(tab=='Day Book') return _dayBook(data);
    if(tab=='Sales Register') return _salesRegister(data);
    if(tab=='Outstandings') return _outstandings(data);
    return const Center(child:Text('Open a module from the dashboard.'));
  }

  /// Outstanding rows are from the Windows invoice settlement snapshot. Keep
  /// both debit and credit balances; do not combine them into a fabricated due.
  Widget _outstandings(Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    final all=records(data,'outstanding');
    final rows=all.where((r)=>'${r['customer']??''}'.toLowerCase().contains(query.toLowerCase()))
      .where((r)=>money(r['signedBalance']).abs()>.005).toList();
    final signed=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+money(r['signedBalance']).toDouble());
    final due=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r){final x=money(r['signedBalance']).toDouble();return sum+(x>0?x:0);});
    final advance=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r){final x=money(r['signedBalance']).toDouble();return sum+(x<0?-x:0);});
    return Column(children:[
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16,10,16,2),child:Row(children:[
        const Expanded(child:Text('Outstanding Accounts',style:TextStyle(
          fontSize:12,color:muted,fontWeight:FontWeight.w800))),
        Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:24,vertical:10),
          decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFF9D1C49),
            borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(22)),
          child:const Text('Arrears',style:TextStyle(color:Colors.white,
            fontSize:12,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
      ])),
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,12,14,5),child:TextField(
        onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>query=v),
        decoration:InputDecoration(hintText:'Search Account',prefixIcon:const Icon(Icons.search),
          filled:true,fillColor:Colors.white,border:OutlineInputBorder(
            borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(17),borderSide:BorderSide.none)))),
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16,6,16,9),child:Row(children:[
        const Icon(Icons.calendar_today_outlined,color:ink,size:18),const SizedBox(width:8),
        Text(dateOnly(data['generatedAt']),style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontWeight:FontWeight.w800)),
        const Spacer(),const Text('Sundry Debtors',style:TextStyle(color:teal,fontWeight:FontWeight.w700)),
      ])),
      Container(margin:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,0,14,12),padding:const EdgeInsets.all(13),
        decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFE7F5F5),
          border:const Border(left:BorderSide(color:teal,width:4)),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(9)),
        child:Column(children:[_totalLine('Total Outstanding', '${rupees(signed.abs())} ${signed<0?'Cr':'Dr'}'),
          const Divider(),_totalLine('Customer Dues',rupees(due)),
          _totalLine('Customer Advances',rupees(advance)),
          Text('${rows.length} accounts · Snapshot only (not a historical date query)',
            style:const TextStyle(fontSize:11,color:muted))])),
      Expanded(child:ListView.builder(itemCount:rows.length,itemBuilder:(context,index){
        final r=rows[index];final b=money(r['signedBalance']).toDouble();
        final name='${r['customer']??'Unknown customer'}';
        return _listCard(title:name,subtitle:'Customer',
          amount:'${rupees(b.abs())} ${b<0?'Cr':'Dr'}',
          onTap:()=>_openRecord('Outstanding',r,data));
      })),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _dayBook(Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    final entries=records(data,'dayBook').where((r){
      return (query.isEmpty||r.values.join(' ').toLowerCase().contains(query.toLowerCase())) &&
        WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate);
    }).toList();
    final dr=entries.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['debit']).toDouble());
    final cr=entries.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['credit']).toDouble());
    Widget td(String v,{int flex=2,bool header=false})=>Expanded(flex:flex,
      child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:5),child:Text(v,
        maxLines:header?2:4,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis,
        style:TextStyle(fontSize:header?12:11,color:header?Colors.white:ink,
          fontWeight:header?FontWeight.w900:FontWeight.normal))));
    final tableRows=<Widget>[];
    String? previousDate;
    for(final r in entries){
      final currentDate='${r['date']??''}';
      if(previousDate!=currentDate){
        tableRows.add(Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(13,10,13,10),
          margin:const EdgeInsets.only(top:3),color:const Color(0xFFE3F2F4),
          child:Text(dateOnly(currentDate),style:const TextStyle(
            fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink,fontSize:12))));
        previousDate=currentDate;
      }
      tableRows.add(InkWell(onTap:()=>_openRecord('Day Book',r,data),
        child:Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:13),
          decoration:const BoxDecoration(border:Border(bottom:BorderSide(color:Color(0xFFDFEBEF)))),
          child:Row(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
            td('${r['type']??'Entry'}'),
            td('${r['customer']??''}',flex:3),td('${r['details']??r['narration']??''}',flex:4),
            td(money(r['debit'])==0?'-':money(r['debit']).toStringAsFixed(2)),
            td(money(r['credit'])==0?'-':money(r['credit']).toStringAsFixed(2)),
          ]))));
    }
    return Column(children:[
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(13,12,13,0),child:Align(alignment:Alignment.centerLeft,
        child:Text('${(data['seller'] is Map?(data['seller'] as Map)['name']:null)??'KUMAR FOODS PROCESSING COMPANY'}',
          style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontSize:16,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)))),
      _filters(),
      Expanded(child:LayoutBuilder(builder:(ctx,box)=>SingleChildScrollView(
        scrollDirection:Axis.horizontal,child:SizedBox(width:box.maxWidth<640?640:box.maxWidth,
        child:ListView(children:[
          Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:13,horizontal:8),
            decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFF008A8C),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(11)),
            child:Row(children:[td('Type',header:true),td('Acc Name',flex:3,header:true),
              td('Description',flex:4,header:true),td('Debit',header:true),td('Credit',header:true)])),
          ...tableRows,
          if(entries.isEmpty)const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(18),child:Text('No Day Book records in this date range.')),
        ]))))),
      Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:15,vertical:12),
        decoration:const BoxDecoration(color:Color(0xFFE2F3F6),border:Border(top:BorderSide(color:teal))),
        child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
          const Text('Summary',style:TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink)),
          _totalLine('Dr',rupees(dr)),_totalLine('Cr',rupees(cr)),
        ])),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _receipts(Map<String,dynamic> data){
    final all=[...records(data,'receipts').map((r)=>{...r,'_kind':'Receipt'}),
      ...records(data,'payments').map((r)=>{...r,'_kind':'Payment'})];
    final periodRows=all.where((r){
      final search=query.isEmpty||r.values.join(' ').toLowerCase().contains(query.toLowerCase());
      return search && WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate);
    }).toList();
    final rows=periodRows.where((r)=>r['_kind']==receiptFilter).toList();
    final receipts=periodRows.where((r)=>r['_kind']=='Receipt').toList();
    final payments=periodRows.where((r)=>r['_kind']=='Payment').toList();
    return Column(children:[Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:12,vertical:7),child:Row(children:[
      Expanded(child:ChoiceChip(label:const Text('↓  Receipt'),selected:receiptFilter=='Receipt',
        selectedColor:const Color(0xFFA1E0DE),onSelected:(_)=>setState(()=>receiptFilter='Receipt'))),
      const SizedBox(width:8),Expanded(child:ChoiceChip(label:const Text('↑  Payment'),
        selected:receiptFilter=='Payment',selectedColor:const Color(0xFFFFDDA4),
        onSelected:(_)=>setState(()=>receiptFilter='Payment'))),
    ])),_filters(),Expanded(child:ListView(children:[
      ...rows.map((r)=>Card(margin:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,6,14,10),color:Colors.white,elevation:0,
        shape:RoundedRectangleBorder(side:const BorderSide(color:Color(0xFFD9E7E8)),
          borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(18)),
        child:InkWell(onTap:()=>_openRecord('${r['_kind']}',r,data),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(18),
        child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(16),child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
          Row(children:[Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(8),decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFE5F7F5),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(9)),
            child:Text('${r['_kind']}'.toUpperCase(),style:const TextStyle(color:teal,fontSize:11,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
            const SizedBox(width:8),Text(dateOnly(r['date']),style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w600,color:muted)),
            const SizedBox(width:4),Expanded(child:Align(alignment:Alignment.centerRight,child:FittedBox(fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,child:Text(rupees(r['amount']),style:const TextStyle(color:teal,fontSize:19,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))))),
          ]),const SizedBox(height:9),Text('${r['customer']??r['account']??''}',style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:17)),
          const SizedBox(height:12),Row(children:[Expanded(child:Text('Mode\n${r['mode']??'Cash'}')),Expanded(child:Text('Amount\n${rupees(r['amount'])}',style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w700))),Expanded(child:Text('Discount\n${rupees(r['discount'])}'))]),
        ]))))),
      if(rows.isEmpty)const Center(child:Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(20),child:Text('No receipts or payments in this period.')))
    ])),Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(14),child:Column(children:[
      _totalLine('Receipt Total',rupees(receipts.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['amount']).toDouble()))),
      _totalLine('Payment Total',rupees(payments.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['amount']).toDouble()))),
      const Text('Only posted Office ERP entries · Read-only',style:TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:11)),
    ]))]);
  }

  Widget _accounts(Map<String,dynamic> data,{bool forLedger=false}) {
    final rows=records(data,'customers').where((row)=>
      '${row['name']} ${row['code']} ${row['mobile']}'.toLowerCase().contains(query.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return Column(children:[
      _filters(showDates:true),
      if(!forLedger)const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,0,14,7),
        child:Text('Tap a customer to see entries for the selected dates. From–To dates filter the ledger; balances here are the latest Windows snapshot.',
        style:TextStyle(fontSize:11,color:muted))),
      Expanded(child:ListView.builder(itemCount:rows.length,itemBuilder:(context,index){
        final row=rows[index];
        final cid=money(row['id']).toInt();
        final match=records(data,'outstanding').where((a)=>money(a['customerId']).toInt()==cid).toList();
        final bal=match.isEmpty?null:money(match.first['signedBalance']).toDouble();
        final history=WindowsMirrorData.customerHistory(data,cid);
        final visibleCount=history.where((r)=>WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate)).length;
        final filtered=fromDate!=null||toDate!=null;
        return _listCard(title:'${row['name']}',
          subtitle:'Customer${row['mobile']==null?'':' · ${row['mobile']}'}',
          detail:filtered?'$visibleCount of ${history.length} entries in selected dates'
            :'${history.length} total posted entries · View full ledger',
          amount:bal==null?'':'${rupees(bal.abs())} ${bal<0?'Cr':'Dr'}',
          onTap:()=>openTab('Account Ledger',id:cid,name:'${row['name']}',keepDates:true));
      })),
    ]);
  }

  /// Ledger is calculated from posted Windows journal entries, not from a UI-total
  /// or a settlement-only balance. Date grouping is chronological (id tie-break).
  Widget _ledger(Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    final customer=records(data,'customers').where((r)=>money(r['id']).toInt()==accountId).toList();
    final selected=customer.isEmpty?<String,dynamic>{'name':accountName}:customer.first;
    final all=WindowsMirrorData.customerHistory(data,accountId??-1);
    bool inDate(Map<String,dynamic> r)=>WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate);
    // For date-specific statements, opening balance = all prior journal movement.
    final opening=WindowsMirrorData.periodOpening(all,fromDate);
    final visible=all.where(inDate).toList();
    final debits=visible.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['debit']).toDouble());
    final credits=visible.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['credit']).toDouble());
    final closing=opening+debits-credits;
    final settlement=records(data,'outstanding').where((r)=>money(r['customerId']).toInt()==accountId).toList();
    final invoiceBalance=settlement.isEmpty?null:money(settlement.first['signedBalance']).toDouble();
    // UI and PDF consume the same computed statement.
    final ledgerData=LedgerStatementData(company:'${(data['seller'] is Map?(data['seller'] as Map)['name']:null)??'KUMAR FOODS PROCESSING COMPANY'}',
      account:'${selected['name']??accountName}',phone:'${selected['mobile']??''}',
      from:fromDate,to:toDate,opening:opening,entries:visible);
    final grouped=<String,List<Map<String,dynamic>>>{};
    for(final r in visible){grouped.putIfAbsent(WindowsMirrorData.voucherDay(r['date']),()=>[]).add(r);}
    final groups=grouped.entries.toList();
    double running=opening;
    final groupWidgets=<Widget>[];
    for(final group in groups){
      final groupDr=group.value.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['debit']).toDouble());
      final groupCr=group.value.fold<double>(0,(v,r)=>v+money(r['credit']).toDouble());
      groupWidgets.add(Container(margin:const EdgeInsets.only(bottom:8),padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:14,vertical:11),
        decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFDDF3F1),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        child:Row(children:[Expanded(child:Text(dateOnly(group.key),style:const TextStyle(color:teal,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:15))),
        Flexible(child:FittedBox(fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,alignment:Alignment.centerRight,child:Text('Dr ${rupees(groupDr)}   Cr ${rupees(groupCr)}',style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontSize:11,fontWeight:FontWeight.w800))))])));
      for(final r in group.value){
        final dr=money(r['debit']).toDouble(),cr=money(r['credit']).toDouble();
        running+=dr-cr;
        final balance=running;
        groupWidgets.add(InkWell(onTap:()=>_openRecord('Ledger',r,data),
          borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(19),
          child:Container(margin:const EdgeInsets.only(bottom:10),padding:const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          decoration:BoxDecoration(color:Colors.white,border:Border.all(color:const Color(0xFFD7E7E5)),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(19)),
          child:Row(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
            Expanded(child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
              Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:9,vertical:5),decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFF8E9EC),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                child:Text('${r['referenceType']??'Voucher'}',style:const TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF8E2243),fontWeight:FontWeight.w800,fontSize:12))),
              const SizedBox(height:8),Text('${r['narration']??r['account']??''}',style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontSize:13.5)),
            ])),const SizedBox(width:8),Container(width:(MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width*.36).clamp(106.0,138.0).toDouble(),padding:const EdgeInsets.all(9),
              decoration:BoxDecoration(color:pageBg,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(12)),child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
                Text(dr>0?'Debit':'Credit',style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:11)),
                FittedBox(fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,alignment:Alignment.centerLeft,child:Text(rupees(dr>0?dr:cr),style:TextStyle(color:dr>0?const Color(0xFF14803B):const Color(0xFFAA2C40),fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:15))),
                const Divider(),const Text('Balance',style:TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:11)),
                FittedBox(fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,alignment:Alignment.centerLeft,child:Text('${rupees(balance.abs())} ${balance<0?'Cr':'Dr'}',style:const TextStyle(color:Color(0xFFAE7D13),fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:12))),
              ]))]),))); 
      }
    }
    return Column(children:[Expanded(child:ListView(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,8,14,8),children:[
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:5,vertical:8),
        child:Row(children:[const Icon(Icons.history,color:teal,size:20),const SizedBox(width:7),
        Expanded(child:Text(fromDate==null&&toDate==null
          ? 'Complete customer history: ${all.length} entries'
          : 'Selected period: ${visible.length} of ${all.length} entries',
          style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:13)))])),
      Container(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(17),decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFE7F3F5),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
          Text('${selected['name']??accountName}',style:const TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF932044),fontSize:19,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
          Text('Mobile: ${selected['mobile']??''}',style:const TextStyle(color:muted)),const SizedBox(height:6),
          Text('Displayed vouchers: ${visible.length} / ${all.length}',style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontWeight:FontWeight.w700,fontSize:12)),const SizedBox(height:9),
          Text('Op Bal: ${rupees(opening.abs())} ${opening<0?'Cr':'Dr'}',style:const TextStyle(color:teal,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
          const SizedBox(height:13),Row(children:[
            Expanded(child:OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_selectDate(true),icon:const Icon(Icons.event),label:Text(fromDate==null?'From date':dateOnly(fromDate!.toIso8601String()),maxLines:1,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis))),
            const SizedBox(width:6),Expanded(child:OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_selectDate(false),icon:const Icon(Icons.event),label:Text(toDate==null?'To date':dateOnly(toDate!.toIso8601String()),maxLines:1,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis))),
          ]),const SizedBox(height:5),
          Row(children:[Expanded(child:FilledButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_ledgerExportOptions(ledgerData),icon:const Icon(Icons.print_rounded),label:const Text('Print / WhatsApp PDF'))),
            IconButton(onPressed:()=>setState(_useToday),tooltip:'Today',icon:const Icon(Icons.restart_alt))]),
        ])),const SizedBox(height:10),
      if(groups.isEmpty) const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(24),child:Text('No posted ledger transactions in selected dates.')),
      ...groupWidgets,
      if(invoiceBalance!=null && fromDate==null && toDate==null && (invoiceBalance-closing).abs()>.01)
        const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(12),child:Text('Note: Invoice-wise Outstanding differs from the posted journal ledger. Reconcile in Windows ERP before collecting payment.',
          style:TextStyle(color:Colors.deepOrange,fontSize:12))),
    ])),
    Container(margin:const EdgeInsets.all(12),padding:const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration:BoxDecoration(color:const Color(0xFFE7F3F5),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(15)),child:Column(children:[
        _totalLine('Debit',rupees(debits)),_totalLine('Credit',rupees(credits)),
        const Divider(),_totalLine('Balance','${rupees(closing.abs())} ${closing<0?'Cr':'Dr'}'),
      ])),
    ]);
  }
  Widget _totalLine(String title,String value)=>Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:4),
    child:Row(children:[Expanded(child:Text(title,style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w800,color:ink))),
    Flexible(child:FittedBox(fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,alignment:Alignment.centerRight,child:Text(value,
      style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink))))]));

  void _printCurrentLedger(){
    final data=snapshot ?? <String,dynamic>{};
    if(accountId==null)return;
    final customers=records(data,'customers').where((r)=>money(r['id']).toInt()==accountId).toList();
    final who=customers.isNotEmpty?customers.first:<String,dynamic>{'name':accountName};
    final ledger=WindowsMirrorData.customerHistory(data,accountId!);
    final opening=WindowsMirrorData.periodOpening(ledger,fromDate);
    final filtered=ledger.where((r)=>WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate)).toList();
    final company=(data['seller'] is Map?(data['seller'] as Map)['name']:null)??
      'KUMAR FOODS PROCESSING COMPANY';
    _ledgerExportOptions(LedgerStatementData(company:'$company',
      account:'${who['name']??accountName}',phone:'${who['mobile']??''}',
      from:fromDate,to:toDate,opening:opening,entries:filtered));
  }
  Future<void> _ledgerExportOptions(LedgerStatementData statement) async {
    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(context:context,showDragHandle:true,builder:(sheetCtx)=>
      SafeArea(child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20,4,20,24),child:Column(
        mainAxisSize:MainAxisSize.min,children:[
          const Icon(Icons.check_circle,color:teal,size:40),const SizedBox(height:9),
          const Text('LEDGER',style:TextStyle(color:ink,fontSize:20,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
          const SizedBox(height:5),const Text('Print or share this ledger?',
            style:TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:14)),const SizedBox(height:18),
          Row(children:[Expanded(child:FilledButton.icon(onPressed:(){Navigator.pop(sheetCtx);
            _printLedger(statement);},icon:const Icon(Icons.print),label:const Text('Print'),
            style:FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor:teal,padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:15)))),
            const SizedBox(width:10),Expanded(child:FilledButton.icon(onPressed:(){Navigator.pop(sheetCtx);
              _printLedger(statement);},icon:const Icon(Icons.share_outlined),label:const Text('WhatsApp PDF'),
              style:FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor:const Color(0xFFDDF1F2),
                foregroundColor:ink,padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:15))))]),
          TextButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(sheetCtx),child:const Text('No')),
          const Text('Use the share menu to select WhatsApp or another app.',
            style:TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:10)),
        ]))));
  }

  Future<void> _printLedger(LedgerStatementData statement) async {
    try {final bytes=await LedgerStatementPdf.make(statement);
      if(!mounted)return;
      await _previewPdf(bytes,'${statement.account} Ledger','Kumar_Foods_Ledger_${statement.account.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]'),'_')}.pdf');
    } catch(e){if(mounted)ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('Ledger PDF: $e')));}
  }

  Widget _filters({bool showDates=true}) => Padding(
    padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(13,8,13,10),child:Column(children:[
      if(showDates)Row(children:[
        Expanded(child:_dateChip(fromDate,'From',()=>_selectDate(true))),
        const SizedBox(width:9),
        Expanded(child:_dateChip(toDate,'To',()=>_selectDate(false))),
        if(fromDate!=null||toDate!=null)IconButton(
          tooltip:'Today',onPressed:()=>setState(_useToday),
          icon:const Icon(Icons.restart_alt_rounded,color:teal)),
      ]),
      const SizedBox(height:9),
      TextField(onChanged:(text)=>setState(()=>query=text),
        decoration:InputDecoration(hintText:'Search Account / Entry',prefixIcon:const Icon(Icons.search),
          fillColor:Colors.white,filled:true,contentPadding:const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          border:OutlineInputBorder(borderSide:const BorderSide(color:Color(0xFFD7E1E6)),
            borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(16)))),
    ]));

  Widget _dateChip(DateTime? date,String label,VoidCallback tap)=>Material(
    color:const Color(0xFFE3F0F2),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(12),
    child:InkWell(onTap:tap,borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(12),
      child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:14),
        child:Row(children:[const Icon(Icons.calendar_month,color:ink,size:18),
          const SizedBox(width:7),Expanded(child:Text(date==null?label:dateOnly(date.toIso8601String()),
            maxLines:1,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontWeight:FontWeight.w800,fontSize:12)))]))));

  Future<void> _selectDate(bool from) async {
    final today=DateTime.now();
    final selected=from?fromDate:toDate;
    final initial=selected??(from?toDate:fromDate)??today;
    final d=await showDatePicker(context:context,initialDate:initial,
      firstDate:DateTime(2000),lastDate:DateTime(2100),
      helpText:from?'SELECT FROM DATE':'SELECT TO DATE');
    if(d==null || !mounted)return;
    setState(() {
      if(from) {
        fromDate=DateTime(d.year,d.month,d.day);
        // Date ranges should never silently return zero rows because
        // the user selected the endpoints in reverse order.
        if(toDate!=null && fromDate!.isAfter(toDate!)) toDate=fromDate;
      } else {
        toDate=DateTime(d.year,d.month,d.day);
        if(fromDate!=null && toDate!.isBefore(fromDate!)) fromDate=toDate;
      }
    });
  }

  Widget _listCard({required String title,required String subtitle,required String amount,
    required VoidCallback? onTap,String detail=''})=>Padding(
      padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,4,14,5),child:Material(color:Colors.white,
        borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(17),child:InkWell(onTap:onTap,
          borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(17),child:Container(
          padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:14,vertical:15),
          decoration:BoxDecoration(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(17),
            border:Border.all(color:const Color(0xFFDCE8EB))),
          child:Row(children:[
            CircleAvatar(radius:19,backgroundColor:teal,child:Text(
              title.isEmpty?'?':title.substring(0,1).toUpperCase(),
              style:const TextStyle(color:Colors.white,fontSize:15,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
            const SizedBox(width:13),Expanded(child:Column(
              crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
                Text(title,maxLines:2,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis,style:const TextStyle(
                  fontSize:14.5,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink)),
                const SizedBox(height:4),Text('$subtitle${detail.isEmpty?'':'\n$detail'}',
                  maxLines:3,style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12)),
              ])),
            if(amount.isNotEmpty)...[const SizedBox(width:7),ConstrainedBox(
              constraints:const BoxConstraints(maxWidth:125),child:FittedBox(
                fit:BoxFit.scaleDown,alignment:Alignment.centerRight,
                child:Text(amount,style:const TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF11823F),
                  fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:13.5))))],
          ])))));

  Future<void> _handleSystemBack() async {
    if(tab!='Home') {openTab(tab=='Account Ledger'?_parentTab:(tab=='Connection'||tab=='Security & Login'?'Settings':'Home'),keepDates:tab=='Account Ledger');return;}
    final exit = await showDialog<bool>(context:context,builder:(ctx)=>AlertDialog(
      title:const Text('Exit Kumar Foods ERP?'),
      content:const Text('Your account will be locked the next time you open the app.'),
      actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,false),child:const Text('STAY')),
        FilledButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,true),child:const Text('EXIT'))]));
    if(exit==true) widget.onSignOut();
  }

  void _openRecord(String type,Map<String,dynamic> row,Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    if(type=='Outstanding') {
      final id=money(row['customerId']).toInt();
      if(id>0) {openTab('Account Ledger',id:id,name:'${row['customer']??''}');return;}
    }
    Map<String,dynamic>? linked;
    if(type=='Ledger') {
      final referenceId=(row['referenceId'] as num?)?.toInt();
      final ref='${row['referenceType']??''}'.toLowerCase();
      if(referenceId!=null) {
        final pool = ref.contains('sale')?records(data,'sales')
          :ref.contains('receipt')?records(data,'receipts')
          :ref.contains('payment')?records(data,'payments'):<Map<String,dynamic>>[];
        for(final item in pool) {
          if((item['id'] as num?)?.toInt()==referenceId) {linked=item;break;}
        }
      }
    }
    final linkedRecord=linked;
    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder:(ctx)=>Scaffold(
      appBar:AppBar(title:Text('$type Details')),
      body:ListView(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(16),children:[
        Card(child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(18),child:Column(
          crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
            Text(row['customer']?.toString()??row['account']?.toString()??type,
              style:const TextStyle(fontSize:20,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
            const SizedBox(height:8),
            ...row.entries.where((entry)=>entry.value!=null && entry.key!='invoicePdfBase64').map((entry)=>Padding(
              padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:8),child:Row(
                crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
                  SizedBox(width:130,child:Text(entry.key,style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontWeight:FontWeight.w600))),
                  Expanded(child:SelectableText('${entry.value}',style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w700))),
                ]))),
            const SizedBox(height:12),
            OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_previewEntryPdf(type,row),
              icon:const Icon(Icons.print),label:const Text('PREVIEW / PRINT THIS ENTRY')),
            if(linkedRecord!=null) ...[
              const Divider(),
              const Text('Related posted voucher',style:TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
              ...linkedRecord.entries.where((e)=>e.key!='invoicePdfBase64').map((e)=>Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:5),
                child:Text('${e.key}: ${e.value}'))),
              if('${row['referenceType']??''}'.toLowerCase().contains('sale'))
                OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_shareSalePdf(linkedRecord,data),
                  icon:const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf),label:const Text('WINDOWS INVOICE PDF')),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height:12),
            if(type=='Sale') FilledButton.icon(onPressed:()=>_shareSalePdf(row,data),
              icon:const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf),label:const Text('DOWNLOAD / PREVIEW INVOICE')),
          ]))),
        if(type=='Sale') const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(9),
          child:Text("Invoice PDF is fetched securely from the Windows ERP's original PDF archive, with the same stamp and layout.",
            style:TextStyle(fontSize:12,color:muted))),
      ]),
    )));
  }

  Future<void> _shareSalePdf(Map<String,dynamic> sale,Map<String,dynamic> data) async {
    try {
      // The selected register row carries its OWN original PDF. Do not look
      // up by invoice number alone or reconstruct tax/charges on the phone.
      final original=WindowsMirrorData.originalSaleFor(data,sale);
      if (original==null) {
        throw StateError('Original Windows voucher for this sale was not found. Sync the office PC.');
      }
      late final Uint8List bytes;
      if (WindowsMirrorData.hasOriginalInvoice(original)) {
        bytes=base64Decode((original['invoicePdfBase64'] as String).trim());
      } else {
        final saleNumber='${original['number']??''}'.trim();
        final revision='${original['invoiceRevision']??''}'.trim();
        if (saleNumber.isEmpty || revision.isEmpty) {
          throw StateError('Office PC has not published invoice metadata. Update Windows ERP and Sync Now.');
        }
        if (_pairCode.length<32) throw StateError('Pair the Android app to Windows ERP first.');
        final res=await http.post(
          Uri.parse('$companyUrl/rest/v1/rpc/erp_mobile_invoice_pull'),
          headers:{'apikey':companyPublicKey,'Content-Type':'application/json','Cache-Control':'no-store'},
          body:jsonEncode({'p_pair_code':_pairCode,'p_sale_number':saleNumber,'p_revision':revision}),
        ).timeout(const Duration(seconds:45));
        if (res.statusCode!=200) throw StateError('Private invoice service unavailable (HTTP ${res.statusCode}).');
        final body=jsonDecode(res.body);
        if (body is! Map || body['ready']!=true || body['pdfBase64'] is! String) {
          throw StateError('Original invoice not yet uploaded. Run Windows Sync Now again to send pending PDFs.');
        }
        bytes=base64Decode(body['pdfBase64'] as String);
      }
      if(bytes.length<5 || ascii.decode(bytes.sublist(0,5),allowInvalid:true)!='%PDF-') {
        throw const FormatException('Original Windows PDF is incomplete. Sync again.');
      }
      if(!mounted)return;
      await _previewPdf(bytes,'Invoice ${original['number']??sale['number']??''}',
        '${original['invoiceFilename']??'Kumar_Foods_Invoice.pdf'}');
    } catch(e) {
      if(mounted)ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(
        content:Text('Invoice unavailable: $e'),duration:const Duration(seconds:10)));
    }
  }

  Widget _salesRegister(Map<String,dynamic> data) {
    final all=WindowsMirrorData.sales(data);
    final rows=all.where((r)=>WindowsMirrorData.inPeriod(r,fromDate,toDate) &&
      (query.trim().isEmpty || <dynamic>[r['number'],r['customer'],r['customerMobile'],
        r['product'],r['items'],r['vehicleNo']].join(' ').toLowerCase().contains(query.trim().toLowerCase()))).toList();
    final total=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+money(r['amount']).toDouble());
    final kg=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+money(r['quantity']).toDouble());
    return Column(children:[
      _filters(),
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:15,vertical:5),child:Text(
        'Windows Sales Register · ${rows.length} entries · Last sync ${_syncLabel(context)}',
        style:const TextStyle(fontSize:11,color:muted))),
      Expanded(child:ListView.builder(itemCount:rows.length,itemBuilder:(context,index){
        final r=rows[index];
        final lines=r['items'] is List?(r['items'] as List).whereType<Map>().toList():<Map>[];
        final hasInlinePdf=WindowsMirrorData.hasOriginalInvoice(r);
        final hasPdfPointer='${r['invoiceRevision']??''}'.isNotEmpty;
        return Card(margin:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14,5,14,8),color:Colors.white,elevation:0,
          shape:RoundedRectangleBorder(side:const BorderSide(color:Color(0xFFD9E7E8)),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(18)),

            child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(15),child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
              Row(children:[Expanded(child:Text('${r['number']??''}',style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:teal))),
                Text(dateOnly(r['date']),style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12))]),
              const SizedBox(height:8),Text('${r['customer']??''}',style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:17,color:ink)),
              const SizedBox(height:8),
              if(lines.isEmpty) Text('${r['product']??''} · ${money(r['quantity']).toStringAsFixed(3)} Kg'),

  }

  Future<void> _previewPdf(List<int> bytes,String title,String filename) async {

        canChangeOrientation:false,canChangePageFormat:false,
      )),SafeArea(child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(12),child:Row(children:[
        Expanded(child:OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:()=>Printing.sharePdf(bytes:Uint8List.fromList(bytes),filename:filename),
          icon:const Icon(Icons.share),label:const Text('SAVE / SHARE'))),
        const SizedBox(width:10),Expanded(child:FilledButton.icon(
          onPressed:()=>Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout:(_)async=>Uint8List.fromList(bytes)),
          icon:const Icon(Icons.print),label:const Text('PRINT'))),
      ])))]),
    )));
  }

  Widget _securityPage() {
    final oldPassword=securityOldPassword;
    final newPassword=securityNewPassword;
    final newEmail=securityNewEmail;
    return FutureBuilder<String>(future:LocalGate.email,builder:(ctx,snapshot){
      if(!snapshot.hasData)return const Center(child:CircularProgressIndicator());
      if(newEmail.text.isEmpty)newEmail.text=snapshot.data!;
      return ListView(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(20),children:[
        const Icon(Icons.fingerprint,size:60,color:teal),
        const Text('Login & Biometric Settings',style:TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.bold,fontSize:23)),
        const SizedBox(height:12),Text('Login email: ${snapshot.data}'),
        const SizedBox(height:18),FilledButton.icon(onPressed:()async {
          final password=await _askPassword();if(password==null)return;
          try {await LocalGate.enableBiometric(password);
            if(mounted)ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content:Text('Fingerprint login enabled.')));
          } catch(e){if(mounted)ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('$e')));}
        },icon:const Icon(Icons.fingerprint),label:const Text('ENABLE FINGERPRINT')),
        OutlinedButton(onPressed:()async {await LocalGate.disableBiometric();if(mounted)setState((){});},
          child:const Text('DISABLE FINGERPRINT')),
        const SizedBox(height:22),const Text('Change Email / Password',style:TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.bold)),
        TextField(controller:oldPassword,obscureText:true,decoration:const InputDecoration(labelText:'Current Password')),
        TextField(controller:newEmail,decoration:const InputDecoration(labelText:'New Login Email')),
        TextField(controller:newPassword,obscureText:true,decoration:const InputDecoration(labelText:'New Password (10+ characters)')),
        const SizedBox(height:10),OutlinedButton(onPressed:()async {
          try {await LocalGate.change(currentPassword:oldPassword.text,newEmail:newEmail.text,newPassword:newPassword.text);
            if(mounted){ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content:Text('Credentials updated; biometric login disabled until re-enabled.')));setState((){});}
          }catch(e){if(mounted)ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('$e')));}
        },child:const Text('SAVE LOGIN CHANGES')),
        const SizedBox(height:16),OutlinedButton.icon(onPressed:widget.onSignOut,
          icon:const Icon(Icons.logout),label:const Text('LOCK APP / LOG OUT')),
        const SizedBox(height:8),const Text('Fingerprint is optional and uses biometric enrollment on this Android device. Set a strong password on first launch.'),
      ]);
    });
  }

  Future<String?> _askPassword() async {
    final controller=TextEditingController();
    final value=await showDialog<String>(context:context,builder:(ctx)=>AlertDialog(
      title:const Text('Verify your login password'),
      content:TextField(controller:controller,obscureText:true,autofocus:true,
        decoration:const InputDecoration(labelText:'Current password')),
      actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx),child:const Text('CANCEL')),
        FilledButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,controller.text),child:const Text('VERIFY'))],
    ));
    controller.dispose();return value;
  }

}


========== FILE: D:\New folder (2)\Kumar_Foods_Android_Studio_v3_4\lib\mobile_drafts.dart ==========

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

/// Drafts are never accounting vouchers. The office ERP must validate and POST
/// each proposal atomically before a ledger/stock/cash balance may change.
class MobileDrafts {
 static const vault=FlutterSecureStorage();
 static const storageKey='kf_v3_unposted_mobile_drafts';
 static Future<List<Map<String,dynamic>>> list() async {
   final json=await vault.read(key:storageKey);
   if(json==null || json.isEmpty) return [];
   final value=jsonDecode(json);
   if(value is! List) return [];
   return value.whereType<Map>().map((v)=>Map<String,dynamic>.from(v)).toList();
 }
 static Future<String> save(String type,Map<String,dynamic> values) async {
   if(!['new_customer','receipt','payment'].contains(type)) throw ArgumentError('Unsupported draft');
   final draft=await list();
   final random=Random.secure();
   final nonce=List<int>.generate(16,(_)=>random.nextInt(256));
   final id='mobile_${DateTime.now().toUtc().microsecondsSinceEpoch}_${base64UrlEncode(nonce).replaceAll('=','')}';
   draft.insert(0,{'id':id,'kind':type,'data':values,'createdAt':DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String(),
     'status':'LOCAL_DRAFT_NOT_POSTED'});
   await vault.write(key:storageKey,value:jsonEncode(draft));
   return id;
 }
 static Future<void> delete(String id) async {
   final remaining=(await list()).where((row)=>row['id']!=id).toList();
   await vault.write(key:storageKey,value:jsonEncode(remaining));
 }
}


========== FILE: D:\New folder (2)\Kumar_Foods_Android_Studio_v3_4\lib\session_gate.dart ==========

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'brand.dart';
import 'local_gate.dart';

/// A local app lock. Browser/Chrome uses password; Android can use enrolled
/// biometric authentication. The cloud pairing code is separate from both.
class SessionGate extends StatefulWidget {
  const SessionGate({super.key,required this.viewer});
  final Widget Function(VoidCallback logout) viewer;
  @override State<SessionGate> createState()=>_SessionGateState();
}
class _SessionGateState extends State<SessionGate> {
  bool? initialized;
  bool unlocked=false;
  bool busy=false;
  bool biometricReady=false;
  bool passwordVisible=false;
  bool usePassword=true;
  String error='';
  final email=TextEditingController(text:LocalGate.defaultEmail);
  final password=TextEditingController();
  final repeat=TextEditingController();
  @override void initState(){super.initState();_load();}
  @override void dispose(){email.dispose();password.dispose();repeat.dispose();super.dispose();}
  Future<void> _load() async {
    try {
      final has=await LocalGate.configured;
      final e=await LocalGate.email;
      final enable=has && await LocalGate.biometricEnabled;
      final available=enable && await LocalGate.canUseBiometric();
      if(!mounted)return;
      setState((){initialized=has;email.text=e;biometricReady=available;usePassword=!available;});
      if(available)await _fingerprint();
    } catch(e){if(mounted)setState((){initialized=false;error='Could not open local login settings: $e';});}
  }
  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if(busy)return;
    setState((){busy=true;error='';});
    try {
      if(initialized==false) {
        if(password.text.length<10 || password.text!=repeat.text) {
          throw StateError('Use a 10+ character password and repeat it correctly.');
        }
        await LocalGate.setup(email:email.text,password:password.text);
        if(mounted)setState(()=>initialized=true);
      } else {
        final valid=await LocalGate.verify(email.text,password.text);
        if(!valid)throw StateError('Incorrect password.');
      }
      if(mounted)setState(()=>unlocked=true);
    } catch(e){if(mounted)setState(()=>error=e.toString().replaceFirst('Bad state: ',''));}
    finally{if(mounted)setState(()=>busy=false);}
  }
  Future<void> _fingerprint() async {
    if(!biometricReady){if(mounted)setState(()=>usePassword=true);return;}
    if(mounted)setState((){busy=true;error='';});
    final okay=await LocalGate.biometricUnlock();
    if(!mounted)return;
    setState((){busy=false;unlocked=okay;
      if(!okay){usePassword=true;error='Fingerprint cancelled or unavailable. Enter your password.';}});
  }
  void _logout(){setState((){unlocked=false;password.clear();repeat.clear();error='';
      usePassword=!biometricReady;});}
  @override Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if(unlocked)return widget.viewer(_logout);
    if(initialized==null)return const Scaffold(body:Center(child:CircularProgressIndicator()));
    final isSetup=initialized==false;
    return Scaffold(body:Stack(children:[
      Positioned.fill(child:Image.asset('assets/images/wheat_field_premium.jpg',fit:BoxFit.cover)),
      Positioned.fill(child:Container(decoration:const BoxDecoration(gradient:LinearGradient(
        begin:Alignment.topCenter,end:Alignment.bottomCenter,colors:[
          Color(0xAAFFEDB8),Color(0xA9E1E5C0),Color(0x6BFFF2C2),Color(0xB812544B)])))),
      SafeArea(child:LayoutBuilder(builder:(ctx,limits)=>Center(
        child:ConstrainedBox(constraints:const BoxConstraints(maxWidth:470),
          child:SingleChildScrollView(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:25,vertical:20),
            child:ConstrainedBox(constraints:BoxConstraints(minHeight:limits.maxHeight-40),
              child:Column(mainAxisAlignment:MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,children:[
                Column(children:[
                  const SizedBox(height:12),
                  const KumarLogo(size:105),const SizedBox(height:12),
                  const Text('Kumar Foods',textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:TextStyle(
                    fontSize:34,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:Color(0xFF06464C),height:1.1)),
                  const Text('E R P',textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:TextStyle(
                    fontSize:24,letterSpacing:5,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:Color(0xFF06464C))),
                  const SizedBox(height:3),const Text('Pure Food. Total Control.',
                    style:TextStyle(fontSize:13,color:Color(0xFF15565A),fontWeight:FontWeight.w800)),
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height:25),
                Container(width:double.infinity,padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20,22,20,18),
                  decoration:BoxDecoration(color:Colors.white.withOpacity(.95),
                    borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(27),
                    border:Border.all(color:const Color(0xCCFFFFFF),width:1.6),
                    boxShadow:const [BoxShadow(color:Color(0x38073536),blurRadius:28,offset:Offset(0,12))]),
                  child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,children:[
                    if(!isSetup)...[
                      const CircleAvatar(radius:31,backgroundColor:Color(0xFFDDF7F2),
                        child:Icon(Icons.fingerprint,color:Color(0xFF057D7D),size:43)),
                      const SizedBox(height:13),
                      const Text('Welcome Back',textAlign:TextAlign.center,
                        style:TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF0B3E48),fontSize:21,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
                      const SizedBox(height:4),
                      Text(biometricReady?'Unlock securely with your fingerprint':'Enter your secure password',
                        textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:const TextStyle(
                          color:Color(0xFF72858C),fontSize:12)),
                    ] else ...[
                      const Text('Create your Private Login',textAlign:TextAlign.center,
                        style:TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF0B3E48),fontSize:21,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
                      const SizedBox(height:6),const Text('One-time setup · No default password',
                        textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:TextStyle(color:Color(0xFF72858C),fontSize:12)),
                    ],
                    if(!isSetup&&biometricReady)...[
                      const SizedBox(height:22),FilledButton.icon(onPressed:busy?null:_fingerprint,
                        style:FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor:const Color(0xFF078E90),
                          padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:16),
                          shape:RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(16))),
                        icon:const Icon(Icons.fingerprint,size:26),
                        label:const Text('LOGIN WITH FINGERPRINT',style:TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
                      const SizedBox(height:8),TextButton(onPressed:()=>setState(()=>usePassword=!usePassword),
                        child:Text(usePassword?'HIDE PASSWORD':'USE PASSWORD INSTEAD')),
                    ],
                    if(isSetup||usePassword||!biometricReady)...[
                      const SizedBox(height:17),
                      if(isSetup)TextField(controller:email,keyboardType:TextInputType.emailAddress,
                        decoration:_field('Admin Email',Icons.email_outlined)),
                      if(isSetup)const SizedBox(height:11),
                      TextField(controller:password,obscureText:!passwordVisible,
                        onSubmitted:(_){if(!isSetup)_submit();},
                        decoration:_field(isSetup?'Create password (10+ characters)':'Secure password',
                          Icons.lock_outline).copyWith(suffixIcon:IconButton(
                            icon:Icon(passwordVisible?Icons.visibility_off:Icons.visibility),
                            onPressed:()=>setState(()=>passwordVisible=!passwordVisible)))),
                      if(isSetup)...[
                        const SizedBox(height:11),TextField(controller:repeat,obscureText:true,
                          decoration:_field('Confirm password',Icons.verified_user_outlined)),
                      ],
                      const SizedBox(height:15),FilledButton.icon(onPressed:busy?null:_submit,
                        style:FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor:const Color(0xFF0A8684),
                          padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical:15),
                          shape:RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(15))),
                        icon:Icon(isSetup?Icons.check_circle_outline:Icons.lock_open_rounded),
                        label:Text(isSetup?'CREATE SECURE LOGIN':'UNLOCK ERP',
                          style:const TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
                    ],
                    if(error.isNotEmpty)...[const SizedBox(height:12),Text(error,
                      textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:const TextStyle(
                        color:Color(0xFF9E294E),fontSize:12))],
                  ])),
                const SizedBox(height:22),Container(
                  padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:16,vertical:9),
                  decoration:BoxDecoration(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(20),
                    color:const Color(0xBB064B4E)),
                  child:const Text('आटा  •  पोषण  •  विश्वास',textAlign:TextAlign.center,
                    style:TextStyle(color:Colors.white,fontSize:14,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900))),
                const SizedBox(height:7),const Text('Fingerprint works on Android · Password works on Chrome',
                  textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:TextStyle(color:Colors.white,
                    fontSize:10,fontWeight:FontWeight.w700)),
              ]))))))),
    ]));
  }
  InputDecoration _field(String label,IconData icon)=>InputDecoration(
    labelText:label,prefixIcon:Icon(icon,color:const Color(0xFF078E90)),filled:true,
    fillColor:const Color(0xFFF6FCFC),contentPadding:const EdgeInsets.all(14),
    enabledBorder:OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(13),
      borderSide:const BorderSide(color:Color(0xFFD6E4E4))),
    focusedBorder:OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(13),
      borderSide:const BorderSide(color:Color(0xFF078E90),width:1.6)));
}
