import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Read-only reports from the current Windows ERP cloud snapshot.
/// Do not enter or adjust stock on mobile: Windows is the ledger authority.
class ChokarMobileReports extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChokarMobileReports({super.key, required this.snapshot,
    required this.mode, this.query = '', this.fromDate, this.toDate});
  final Map<String, dynamic> snapshot;
  final String mode;
  final String query;
  final DateTime? fromDate;
  final DateTime? toDate;

  @override
  State<ChokarMobileReports> createState() => _ChokarMobileReportsState();
}

class _ChokarMobileReportsState extends State<ChokarMobileReports> {
  String product = 'All';
  static const teal = Color(0xFF078D8B);
  static const ink = Color(0xFF163C41);
  static const muted = Color(0xFF687E83);

  double _n(dynamic value) {
    final parsed = value is num ? value.toDouble() : double.tryParse('${value ?? ''}');
    return parsed != null && parsed.isFinite ? parsed : 0;
  }
  String _kg(dynamic value) => '${_n(value).toStringAsFixed(3)} kg';
  String _money(dynamic value) => '₹${_n(value).toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _rows(dynamic value) => value is List
      ? value.whereType<Map>().map((v) => Map<String, dynamic>.from(v)).toList()
      : <Map<String, dynamic>>[];

  bool _visible(Map<String, dynamic> row, List<String> searchKeys) {
    final date = DateTime.tryParse('${row['date'] ?? ''}');
    final day = date == null ? null : DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day);
    if (widget.fromDate != null && (day == null ||
        day.isBefore(DateTime(widget.fromDate!.year, widget.fromDate!.month, widget.fromDate!.day)))) return false;
    if (widget.toDate != null && (day == null ||
        day.isAfter(DateTime(widget.toDate!.year, widget.toDate!.month, widget.toDate!.day)))) return false;
    final query = widget.query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return query.isEmpty || searchKeys.any((key) =>
        '${row[key] ?? ''}'.toLowerCase().contains(query));
  }

  Widget _panel({required Widget child}) => Card(
    elevation: 0, color: Colors.white, margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 13, vertical: 5),
    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFD6E5E8))),
    child: Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(13), child: child),
  );
  Widget _label(String name, String value, {Color color = ink}) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
    child: Row(children: [Expanded(child:Text(name, style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12))),
      Flexible(child:Text(value, textAlign:TextAlign.end, style:TextStyle(color:color,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,fontSize:13)))]),
  );
  Widget _heading(String text) => Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(17,12,12,6),
    child:Text(text,style:const TextStyle(fontSize:17,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:ink)));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final raw = widget.snapshot['chokarReport'];
    if (raw is! Map || raw['version'] != 1 || raw['readOnly'] != true) {
      return Center(child:Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child:Text('इस report के लिए Windows ERP 3.5 का नया Sync Now आवश्यक है।\n'
          'पुराने snapshot से Chokar stock का अनुमान नहीं लगाया जाएगा।',
          textAlign:TextAlign.center,style:const TextStyle(color:ink,fontWeight:FontWeight.w700))));
    }
    final report = Map<String,dynamic>.from(raw);
    final total = report['totals'] is Map
        ? Map<String,dynamic>.from(report['totals'] as Map) : <String,dynamic>{};
    final issues = report['issues'] is List ? (report['issues'] as List).map((v)=>'$v').toList() : <String>[];
    return ListView(children:[
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16,13,16,7),child:Row(children:[
        const Icon(Icons.verified_user_outlined,size:19,color:teal),const SizedBox(width:7),
        Expanded(child:Text('Windows ERP Saved Snapshot · Read-only\n'
          'Saved: ${widget.snapshot['generatedAt'] ?? 'unknown'}',
          style:const TextStyle(color:teal,fontWeight:FontWeight.w800,fontSize:12))),
      ])),
      if(issues.isNotEmpty)_panel(child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
        const Text('पुराने stock की जाँच जरूरी है',style:TextStyle(color:Colors.deepOrange,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900)),
        ...issues.map((m)=>Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.only(top:5),child:Text('• $m',
          style:const TextStyle(color:Colors.deepOrange,fontSize:12)))),
      ])),
      if(widget.mode=='production') ..._production(report),
      if(widget.mode=='stock') ..._stock(report,total),
      if(widget.mode=='customers') ..._customers(report),
      const SizedBox(height:30),
    ]);
  }

  List<Widget> _production(Map<String,dynamic> report) {
    final rows=_rows(report['productionRows']).where((r)=>_visible(r,
      const ['number','remarks','date'])).where((r){
        if(product=='Atta') return _n(r['attaKg'])>0;
        if(product=='Chokar') return _n(r['chokarKg'])>0;
        if(product=='Wastage') return _n(r['wastageKg'])>0;
        return true;
      }).toList();
    final atta=rows.fold<double>(0,(s,r)=>s+_n(r['attaKg']));
    final chokar=rows.fold<double>(0,(s,r)=>s+_n(r['chokarKg']));
    final wastage=rows.fold<double>(0,(s,r)=>s+_n(r['wastageKg']));
    return [
      _heading('Production Register · Atta / Chokar / Wastage'),
      Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:13),child:Wrap(spacing:5,children:[
        for(final p in const ['All','Atta','Chokar','Wastage'])
          ChoiceChip(label:Text(p),selected:product==p,onSelected:(_)=>setState(()=>product=p)),
      ])),
      _panel(child:Column(children:[
        _label('Filtered entries', '${rows.length}'),
        _label('Atta Produced', _kg(atta)),
        _label('Chokar Produced', _kg(chokar)),
        _label('Wastage', _kg(wastage)),
      ])),
      if(rows.isEmpty)const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(23),child:Text('इस filter की कोई production entry नहीं है।')),
      // Build large registers lazily: never create every row at once.
      ...rows.take(100).map((r)=>_panel(child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
        Row(children:[Expanded(child:Text('${r['number']}',style:const TextStyle(fontSize:15,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:teal))),
          Text('${r['date']}'.split('T').first,style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12))]),
        const Divider(),
        _label('Wheat',_kg(r['wheatUsed'])),
        _label('Atta',_kg(r['attaKg'])),
        _label('Chokar',_kg(r['chokarKg'])),
        _label('Wastage',_kg(r['wastageKg'])),
        _label('Chokar packed at production',_kg(r['chokarPackedKg'])),
        _label('Chokar loose after production',_kg(r['chokarLooseKg'])),
        if('${r['remarks']??''}'.isNotEmpty)Text('${r['remarks']}',style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12)),
      ]))),
      if(rows.length>100)Padding(padding:const EdgeInsets.all(15),child:Text(
        'Showing first 100 of ${rows.length} entries. Use date/search to narrow results.',
        style:const TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:12))),
    ];
  }

  List<Widget> _stock(Map<String,dynamic> report,Map<String,dynamic> total) {
    final packs=_rows(report['packBalances']);
    final packing=_rows(report['packingRows']).where((r)=>
      widget.query.isEmpty || '${r['id']} ${r['packKg']}'.toLowerCase()
          .contains(widget.query.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return [
      _heading('Chokar Stock · Last Office Sync'),
      _panel(child:Column(children:[
        _label('Total produced',_kg(total['chokarKg'])),
        _label('Packed at Production',_kg(total['productionPackedChokarKg'])),
        _label('Packed later',_kg(total['laterPackedChokarKg'])),
        _label('Sold',_kg(total['soldChokarKg'])),
        const Divider(),
        _label('Loose Chokar remaining',_kg(total['looseChokarKg']),
          color:_n(total['looseChokarKg'])<0?Colors.red:teal),
        _label('Packed Chokar remaining',_kg(total['remainingPackedChokarKg']),
          color:_n(total['remainingPackedChokarKg'])<0?Colors.red:teal),
        if(_n(total['legacyUnknownPackKg'])>0)
          _label('Legacy sales without pack size',_kg(total['legacyUnknownPackKg']),color:Colors.deepOrange),
      ])),
      _heading('Bag size-wise remaining'),
      if(packs.isEmpty)_panel(child:const Text('No packed Chokar recorded.')),
      ...packs.map((r)=>_panel(child:_label('${_n(r['packKg']).toStringAsFixed(3)} Kg bag',
        '${_n(r['bags']).toStringAsFixed(0)} bags',
        color:_n(r['bags'])<0?Colors.red:teal))),
      _heading('Later Chokar Packing Vouchers'),
      if(packing.isEmpty)_panel(child:const Text('No separate packing vouchers yet.')),
      ...packing.take(100).map((p)=>_panel(child:Column(children:[
        _label('Voucher', '${p['id']}'),
        _label('Date','${p['date']}'.split('T').first),
        _label('Pack', '${p['packKg']} Kg × ${p['bags']} bags'),
        _label('Packed total',_kg(p['totalKg'])),
      ]))),
      if(packing.length>100)const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(12),child:Text('First 100 vouchers shown.')),
    ];
  }

  List<Widget> _customers(Map<String,dynamic> report) {
    final lines=_rows(report['salesLines']).where((r)=>_visible(r,
      const ['number','date','customer','source'])).toList();
    final grouped=<String,Map<String,dynamic>>{};
    for(final r in lines) {
      final id='${r['customerId']??''}'.trim();
      final name='${r['customer']??''}'.trim();
      // Never join different customer IDs merely because names are equal.
      final key=id.isNotEmpty && id!='null' ? 'id:$id'
        : 'unverified:${name.toLowerCase()}';
      final group=grouped.putIfAbsent(key,()=>{
        'name':name.isEmpty?'Unidentified customer':name,
        'verifiedId':id.isNotEmpty && id!='null',
        'kg':0.0,'amount':0.0,'lines':0,
      });
      group['kg']=_n(group['kg'])+_n(r['kg']);
      group['amount']=_n(group['amount'])+_n(r['amount']);
      group['lines']=(group['lines'] as int)+1;
    }
    final customers=grouped.values.toList()..sort((a,b)=>
      _n(b['kg']).compareTo(_n(a['kg'])));
    return [
      _heading('Customer-wise Chokar Sales'),
      _panel(child:Column(children:[
        _label('Customers shown','${customers.length}'),
        _label('Chokar sold',_kg(lines.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+_n(r['kg'])))),
        _label('Chokar line amounts',_money(lines.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+_n(r['amount'])))),
        const Text('Mixed Atta + Chokar invoices: only Chokar item lines counted.',
          style:TextStyle(color:muted,fontSize:11)),
      ])),
      for(final r in customers)_panel(child:Column(crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
        Text('${r['name']}',style:const TextStyle(fontSize:16,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:teal)),
        if(r['verifiedId']!=true)
          const Text('Customer ID unverified in old voucher — verify with Windows ERP.',
            style:TextStyle(color:Colors.deepOrange,fontSize:11)),
        _label('Chokar sold',_kg(r['kg'])),
        _label('Amount',_money(r['amount'])),
        _label('Sale lines','${r['lines']}'),
      ])),
      _heading('Chokar Invoice-wise Lines'),
      if(lines.isEmpty)_panel(child:const Text('No Chokar sales match the filters.')),
      for(final r in lines.take(100))_panel(child:Column(children:[
        _label('Sale','${r['number']}  ·  ${r['date']}'.split('T').first),
        _label('Customer','${r['customer']}'),
        _label('Chokar',_kg(r['kg'])),
        _label('Pack','${r['packKg']} Kg × ${r['bags']} bags'),
        _label('Line amount',_money(r['amount'])),
        if(r['legacyUnmatched']==true)const Text('Historical pack size could not be verified.',
          style:TextStyle(color:Colors.deepOrange,fontSize:11)),
      ])),
      if(lines.length>100)const Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(12),
        child:Text('First 100 invoices shown. Use dates/search to narrow results.')),
    ];
  }
}


========== FILE: D:\New folder (2)\Kumar_Foods_Android_Studio_v3_4\lib00000\services\ledger_statement_pdf.dart ==========

import 'windows_mirror_data.dart';
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;

/// Printed ledger follows the reference layout: company ribbon, date bands,
/// opening balance, Dr/Cr/balance summary and running balance by voucher.
/// All numbers are derived from the supplied posted Windows ERP snapshot.
class LedgerStatementData {
  const LedgerStatementData({required this.company, required this.account,
    required this.phone, required this.from, required this.to,
    required this.opening, required this.entries});
  final String company, account, phone;
  final DateTime? from, to;
  final double opening;
  final List<Map<String,dynamic>> entries;
}
class LedgerStatementPdf {
  static final navy=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF13374A);
  static final teal=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF0B817D);
  static final mint=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFDEF4EF);
  static final pale=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFEFF9F8);
  static final yellow=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFFFF8DE);
  static final red=PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF9B254A);

  static double amount(dynamic v)=>v is num?v.toDouble():double.tryParse('$v')??0;
  static String fmt(double v)=>v.abs().toStringAsFixed(2);
  static String sign(double n)=>'Rs.${fmt(n)} ${n<0?'Cr':'Dr'}';
  static String date(DateTime? d)=>d==null?'All available':
    '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}-${d.month.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}-${d.year}';
  static String dateVal(dynamic v){final d=DateTime.tryParse('${v??''}');return d==null?'${v??''}':date(d);}
  static pw.Widget cell(String v,{bool bold=false,int size=8,PdfColor? color})=>pw.Text(v,
    style:pw.TextStyle(fontSize:size.toDouble(),fontWeight:bold?pw.FontWeight.bold:pw.FontWeight.normal,color:color??navy));
  static pw.Widget stripe(String label,double value,PdfColor bg,{String suffix=''})=>pw.Expanded(child:pw.Container(
    padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:10),
    decoration:pw.BoxDecoration(color:bg,border:pw.Border.all(color:teal,width:.35)),
    child:pw.Column(crossAxisAlignment:pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
      cell(label,size:8),pw.SizedBox(height:4),cell('Rs.${fmt(value)}${suffix.isEmpty?'':' $suffix'}',bold:true,size:12),
    ])));
  static pw.Widget dateBand(String date,double dr,double cr)=>pw.Container(
    color:mint,padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:7),
    child:pw.Row(children:[pw.Expanded(flex:5,child:cell(date,bold:true,size:8)),
      pw.Expanded(flex:3,child:cell(dr==0?'':'Dr ${fmt(dr)}',size:8)),
      pw.Expanded(flex:3,child:cell(cr==0?'':'Cr ${fmt(cr)}',size:8))]));
  static pw.Widget voucherRow(Map<String,dynamic> entry,double running)=>pw.Container(
    padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:9),
    decoration:const pw.BoxDecoration(border:pw.Border(bottom:pw.BorderSide(color:PdfColors.grey300,width:.5))),
    child:pw.Row(crossAxisAlignment:pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
      pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:cell(dateVal(entry['date']))),
      pw.Expanded(flex:8,child:pw.Column(crossAxisAlignment:pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
        cell('${entry['referenceType']??'Entry'}',bold:true,size:8),
        cell('${entry['narration']??''}',size:8)])),
      pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:cell(amount(entry['debit'])==0?'-':fmt(amount(entry['debit'])))),
      pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:cell(amount(entry['credit'])==0?'-':fmt(amount(entry['credit'])),color:red)),
      pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:cell('${fmt(running)} ${running<0?'Cr':'Dr'}')),
    ]));
  static Future<Uint8List> make(LedgerStatementData input) async {
    final rows=WindowsMirrorData.ledgerOrder(input.entries);
    final totalDr=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+amount(r['debit']));
    final totalCr=rows.fold<double>(0,(sum,r)=>sum+amount(r['credit']));
    final closing=input.opening+totalDr-totalCr;
    final groups=<String,List<Map<String,dynamic>>>{};
    for(final r in rows){groups.putIfAbsent(WindowsMirrorData.voucherDay(r['date']),()=>[]).add(r);}
    var running=input.opening;
    final ledgerRows=<pw.Widget>[];
    for(final group in groups.entries){
      final debit=group.value.fold<double>(0,(a,r)=>a+amount(r['debit']));
      final credit=group.value.fold<double>(0,(a,r)=>a+amount(r['credit']));
      ledgerRows.add(dateBand(dateVal(group.key),debit,credit));
      for(final record in group.value){
        running+=amount(record['debit'])-amount(record['credit']);
        ledgerRows.add(voucherRow(record,running));
      }
    }
    final doc=pw.Document();
    doc.addPage(pw.MultiPage(
      pageFormat:PdfPageFormat.a4,
      margin:const pw.EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(38,33,38,38),
      header:(ctx)=>pw.Column(children:[
        pw.Container(color:navy,padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.all(11),child:pw.Row(
          mainAxisAlignment:pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,children:[
            pw.Column(crossAxisAlignment:pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
              pw.Text(input.company.toUpperCase(),style:pw.TextStyle(fontWeight:pw.FontWeight.bold,fontSize:13,color:PdfColors.white)),
              pw.Text('ACCOUNT LEDGER',style:const pw.TextStyle(fontSize:9,color:PdfColors.white)),
            ]),pw.Text(date(DateTime.now()),style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8)),
          ])),pw.Container(height:4,color:PdfColors.amber),pw.SizedBox(height:8),
      ]),
      footer:(ctx)=>pw.Row(mainAxisAlignment:pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,children:[
        cell('Generated by Kumar Foods ERP · Mobile read-only ledger',size:7),
        cell('Page ${ctx.pageNumber} of ${ctx.pagesCount}',size:7),
      ]),
      build:(ctx)=>[
        pw.Container(color:pale,padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.all(12),child:pw.Column(
          crossAxisAlignment:pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,children:[
            cell('ACCOUNT',size:8),cell(input.account,bold:true,size:12),
            if(input.phone.isNotEmpty) cell('Mobile: ${input.phone}',size:8),
            pw.SizedBox(height:8),cell('PERIOD: ${date(input.from)} to ${date(input.to)}',size:9),
            pw.SizedBox(height:6),cell('OPENING BALANCE: ${sign(input.opening)}',bold:true,size:11),
          ])),pw.SizedBox(height:12),
        pw.Row(children:[stripe('DEBIT',totalDr,yellow),pw.SizedBox(width:8),
          stripe('CREDIT',totalCr,yellow),pw.SizedBox(width:8),
          stripe('BALANCE',closing.abs(),pale,suffix:closing<0?'Cr':'Dr')]),
        pw.SizedBox(height:10),
        pw.Container(color:red,padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:7),child:pw.Row(children:[
          pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:pw.Text('Date',style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8))),
          pw.Expanded(flex:8,child:pw.Text('Description',style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8))),
          pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:pw.Text('Debit',style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8))),
          pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:pw.Text('Credit',style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8))),
          pw.Expanded(flex:2,child:pw.Text('Balance',style:const pw.TextStyle(color:PdfColors.white,fontSize:8))),
        ])),
        // Use spread across groups. MultiPage flows through many pages naturally.
        ...ledgerRows,
        if(rows.isEmpty)pw.Padding(padding:const pw.EdgeInsets.all(12),child:cell('No posted entries within the selected period.')),
        pw.SizedBox(height:15),pw.Align(alignment:pw.Alignment.centerRight,
          child:cell('CLOSING BALANCE: ${sign(closing)}',bold:true,size:11)),
        pw.SizedBox(height:12),cell('Statement based on the last successfully synchronized Office ERP journal snapshot. '
          'Opening balance is the prior posted journal movement; verify historical opening entries in the Windows ERP.',size:7),
      ]));
    return doc.save();
  }
}


========== FILE: D:\New folder (2)\Kumar_Foods_Android_Studio_v3_4\lib00000\services\windows_mirror_data.dart ==========

/// Pure read-only selectors. Never merge parties by name, renumber vouchers,
/// recalculate stored invoice totals, or deduplicate posted rows on the phone.
class WindowsMirrorData {
  static List<Map<String, dynamic>> rows(Map<String, dynamic> data, String key) {
    final raw = data[key];
    return raw is List
        ? raw.whereType<Map>().map((r) => Map<String, dynamic>.from(r)).toList()
        : <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
  }

  // Match Windows date filters using the saved voucher's calendar date.
  // Do not convert a business date to the phone's timezone.
  static DateTime? businessDate(dynamic value) {
    final match = RegExp(r'^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})').firstMatch('$value');
    if (match == null) return null;
    final year = int.parse(match[1]!);
    final month = int.parse(match[2]!);
    final day = int.parse(match[3]!);
    final date = DateTime(year, month, day);
    return date.year == year && date.month == month && date.day == day ? date : null;
  }

  static bool inPeriod(Map<String, dynamic> row, DateTime? from, DateTime? to) {
    if (from == null && to == null) return true;
    final date = businessDate(row['date']);
    if (date == null) return false;
    return (from == null || !date.isBefore(DateTime(from.year, from.month, from.day))) &&
        (to == null || !date.isAfter(DateTime(to.year, to.month, to.day)));
  }

  static List<Map<String, dynamic>> sales(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    // Windows Sales Register uses app_state Standard Sale vouchers in saved
    // order. SQL sales is a projection and must not be mixed into this list.
    final key = data['salesRegister'] is List ? 'salesRegister'
        : data['standardSales'] is List ? 'standardSales' : 'sales';
    return rows(data, key).map((r) => <String, dynamic>{
      ...r,
      'customer': r['customerName'] ?? r['customer'] ?? '',
      'amount': r['totalAmount'] ?? r['amount'],
    }).toList();
  }

  /// All posted journal entries for exactly one account ID. Do not use
  /// last/first, unique-by-date, or merge different customers by name.
  static List<Map<String, dynamic>> customerHistory(
      Map<String, dynamic> data, int customerId) {
    return ledgerOrder(rows(data, 'ledger').where((entry) {
      final id = num.tryParse('${entry['accountId'] ?? ''}');
      return '${entry['accountType'] ?? ''}'.trim().toLowerCase() == 'customer' &&
          id != null && id == customerId;
    }).toList());
  }

  /// Keep calendar dates as saved on the Windows vouchers (not phone timezone).
  static String voucherDay(dynamic value) {
    final d = businessDate(value);
    if (d == null) return '${value ?? ''}';
    return '${d.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
        '${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
        '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  static double periodOpening(List<Map<String, dynamic>> orderedHistory, DateTime? from) {
    if (from == null) return 0;
    return orderedHistory.where((row) {
      final d = businessDate(row['date']);
      return d != null && d.isBefore(DateTime(from.year,from.month,from.day));
    }).fold<double>(0,(sum,row) => sum +
        (num.tryParse('${row['debit'] ?? 0}') ?? 0).toDouble() -
        (num.tryParse('${row['credit'] ?? 0}') ?? 0).toDouble());
  }

  static List<Map<String, dynamic>> ledgerOrder(List<Map<String, dynamic>> input) {
    final indexed = input.asMap().entries.toList();
    indexed.sort((a, b) {
      // Business date determines chronological history even when Windows has
      // a separate sequence. Multiple vouchers on the same date are retained.
      final dateOrder = voucherDay(a.value['date']).compareTo(voucherDay(b.value['date']));
      if (dateOrder != 0) return dateOrder;
      final asq = a.value['statementSequence'];
      final bsq = b.value['statementSequence'];
      if (asq is num && bsq is num) {
        final sequenceOrder = asq.compareTo(bsq);
        if (sequenceOrder != 0) return sequenceOrder;
      }
      return a.key.compareTo(b.key);
    });
    return indexed.map((e) => e.value).toList();
  }

  static bool olderThan(Map<String, dynamic> incoming, Map<String, dynamic>? current) {
    final next = DateTime.tryParse('${incoming['generatedAt'] ?? ''}');
    final previous = DateTime.tryParse('${current?['generatedAt'] ?? ''}');
    return next != null && previous != null && next.isBefore(previous);
  }

  /// Fail closed when a cloud payload is internally inconsistent. Posted
  /// Windows vouchers remain the authority: the phone must not repair totals.
  /// This does not prove that the office PC has uploaded its newest database.
  static List<String> audit(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final errors = <String>[];
    if (data['schema'] != 1) errors.add('Unsupported office snapshot version.');
    for (final key in const ['customers', 'sales', 'ledger', 'outstanding', 'receipts', 'payments']) {
      if (data[key] is! List) errors.add('Missing $key from the office snapshot.');
    }
    if (errors.isNotEmpty) return errors;

    double? amount(dynamic value) {
      if (value is num) return value.toDouble().isFinite ? value.toDouble() : null;
      if (value is String) return double.tryParse(value.trim());
      return null;
    }
    int? identity(dynamic value) {
      if (value is int) return value;
      if (value is num && value.isFinite && value == value.roundToDouble()) return value.toInt();
      return int.tryParse('$value');
    }
    final customers = rows(data, 'customers');
    final customerIds = <int>{};
    for (final c in customers) {
      final id = identity(c['id']);
      if (id == null || !customerIds.add(id)) {
        errors.add('Invalid or duplicate customer ID in cloud data.');
        break;
      }
    }

    final journalBalances = <int, double>{};
    for (final e in rows(data, 'ledger')) {
      if ('${e['accountType'] ?? ''}'.toLowerCase() != 'customer') continue;
      final id = identity(e['accountId']);
      final dr = amount(e['debit']);
      final cr = amount(e['credit']);
      if (id == null || dr == null || cr == null) {
        errors.add('Ledger contains an invalid amount or customer ID.');
        break;
      }
      journalBalances[id] = (journalBalances[id] ?? 0) + dr - cr;
    }
    for (final e in rows(data, 'outstanding')) {
      final id = identity(e['customerId']);
      final balance = amount(e['signedBalance']);
      if (id == null || balance == null || !customerIds.contains(id)) {
        errors.add('An outstanding balance has an invalid customer or amount.');
        break;
      }
      if (((journalBalances[id] ?? 0) - balance).abs() > 0.06) {
        errors.add('Ledger and Outstanding do not match for a customer.');
        break;
      }
    }

    final sourceSales = data['salesRegister'] is List
        ? rows(data, 'salesRegister')
        : data['standardSales'] is List ? rows(data, 'standardSales') : <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
    final sqlSales = rows(data, 'sales');
    if (sourceSales.isNotEmpty) {
      final byNumber = <String, double>{};
      for (final e in sqlSales) {
        final number = '${e['number'] ?? ''}'.trim();
        final total = amount(e['amount']);
        if (number.isEmpty || total == null || byNumber.containsKey(number)) {
          errors.add('Invalid or duplicated SQL sale voucher.');
          break;
        }
        byNumber[number] = total;
      }
      final seen = <String>{};
      for (final e in sourceSales) {
        final number = '${e['number'] ?? ''}'.trim();
        final total = amount(e['totalAmount'] ?? e['amount']);
        if (number.isEmpty || total == null || !seen.add(number)) {
          errors.add('Invalid or duplicated Windows sale voucher.');
          break;
        }
        final projected = byNumber[number];
        if (projected == null || (total - projected).abs() > 0.06) {
          errors.add('Sales Register and posted sales differ. Sync Windows ERP again.');
          break;
        }
      }
      if (seen.length != sqlSales.length && errors.isEmpty) {
        errors.add('Some sales were omitted from the Windows snapshot.');
      }
    }
    // ERP 3.5 companion reports are optional for older offices. Validate only
    // their transport structure: legacy negative stock is a warning, NOT a
    // reason to erase the last verified snapshot.
    final chokar = data['chokarReport'];
    if (chokar != null) {
      if (chokar is! Map || chokar['version'] != 1 ||
          chokar['readOnly'] != true || chokar['totals'] is! Map ||
          chokar['productionRows'] is! List ||
          chokar['packingRows'] is! List ||
          chokar['salesLines'] is! List ||
          chokar['packBalances'] is! List ||
          chokar['issues'] is! List) {
        errors.add('Invalid Chokar report in office snapshot. Sync from Windows ERP 3.5 again.');
      } else {
        final totals = chokar['totals'] as Map;
        final produced = amount(totals['chokarKg']);
        final packed = amount(totals['packedChokarKg']);
        final loose = amount(totals['looseChokarKg']);
        final sold = amount(totals['soldChokarKg']);
        final remaining = amount(totals['remainingPackedChokarKg']);
        final productionPacked = amount(totals['productionPackedChokarKg']);
        final laterPacked = amount(totals['laterPackedChokarKg']);
        if ([produced, packed, loose, sold, remaining,
             productionPacked, laterPacked].any((value) => value == null) ||
            ((packed ?? 0) - (productionPacked ?? 0) - (laterPacked ?? 0)).abs() > .006 ||
            ((packed ?? 0) - (sold ?? 0) - (remaining ?? 0)).abs() > .006 ||
            ((produced ?? 0) - (packed ?? 0) - (loose ?? 0)).abs() > .006) {
          errors.add('Chokar totals are inconsistent. Keep the previous verified snapshot.');
        }
      }
    }
    return errors.toSet().toList();
  }

  static bool hasOriginalInvoice(Map<String, dynamic> sale) {
    final pdf = sale['invoicePdfBase64'];
    return pdf is String && pdf.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  /// A Sales Register voucher is the authority for invoice rendering, not the
  /// SQL voucher projection, which may intentionally omit PDF and line detail.
  static Map<String, dynamic>? originalSaleFor(
      Map<String, dynamic> data, Map<String, dynamic> row) {
    if (hasOriginalInvoice(row)) return row;
    final number = '${row['number'] ?? ''}'.trim();
    if (number.isEmpty) return null;
    final matches = sales(data).where((e) => '${e['number'] ?? ''}'.trim() == number).toList();
    return matches.length == 1 ? matches.single : null;
  }

}

/// Resolution-independent Vedposh wheat / fresh roti emblem.
/// The dashboard choice can be changed in Settings without an app rebuild.
class KumarLogo extends StatelessWidget {
  const KumarLogo({super.key,this.style='wheat_roti',this.size=84});
  final String style;
  final double size;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context)=>SizedBox.square(dimension:size,
    child:CustomPaint(painter:_KumarLogoPainter(style)));
}
class _KumarLogoPainter extends CustomPainter {
  _KumarLogoPainter(this.style);
  final String style;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final sx=size.width/100, sy=size.height/100;
    canvas.save();canvas.scale(sx,sy);
    final bg=Paint()..shader=const LinearGradient(
      colors:[Color(0xFF053F49),Color(0xFF108883)],
      begin:Alignment.topLeft,end:Alignment.bottomRight,
    ).createShader(const Rect.fromLTWH(0,0,100,100));
    canvas.drawRRect(RRect.fromRectAndRadius(const Rect.fromLTWH(2,2,96,96),const Radius.circular(26)),bg);
    canvas.drawCircle(const Offset(50,50),42,Paint()..color=const Color(0x17FFE5A5));
    final stem=Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFFFD888)..strokeWidth=2.8
      ..strokeCap=StrokeCap.round..style=PaintingStyle.stroke;
    canvas.drawLine(const Offset(34,77),const Offset(45,18),stem);
    final seed=Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFFFD888);
    for(var i=0;i<5;i++){
      final y=19.0+i*9;
      canvas.save();canvas.translate(40,y);canvas.rotate(-.6);
      canvas.drawOval(const Rect.fromLTWH(-12,-3,18,8),seed);canvas.restore();
      canvas.save();canvas.translate(46,y+4);canvas.rotate(.55);
      canvas.drawOval(const Rect.fromLTWH(-2,-3,15,7),seed);canvas.restore();
    }
    if(style=='minimal'){
      final paragraph=TextPainter(text:const TextSpan(text:'KF',style:TextStyle(
        fontSize:31,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:Colors.white)),textDirection:TextDirection.ltr);
      paragraph.layout();paragraph.paint(canvas,const Offset(48,42));
    } else if(style=='wheat_roti') {
      canvas.drawOval(const Rect.fromLTWH(43,45,46,36),Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFFCE0A7));
      canvas.drawOval(const Rect.fromLTWH(43,45,46,36),Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFBD8845)
        ..style=PaintingStyle.stroke..strokeWidth=2);
      for(final p in <Offset>[const Offset(57,55),const Offset(73,63),const Offset(64,73)]){
        canvas.save();canvas.translate(p.dx,p.dy);canvas.rotate(math.pi/8);
        canvas.drawOval(const Rect.fromLTWH(0,0,7,4),Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFD79A55));canvas.restore();
      }
    } else {
      final t=TextPainter(text:const TextSpan(text:'KF',style:TextStyle(
        fontSize:27,fontWeight:FontWeight.w900,color:Color(0xFFFFE5A1))),textDirection:TextDirection.ltr);
      t.layout();t.paint(canvas,const Offset(51,47));
    }
    canvas.restore();
  }
  @override bool shouldRepaint(covariant _KumarLogoPainter old)=>old.style!=style;
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:local_auth/local_auth.dart';

/// A device-local app lock. It does not replace server-side authorization.
/// No default hard-coded password is shipped in an APK.
class LocalGate {
  static const store = FlutterSecureStorage();
  static final LocalAuthentication biometric = LocalAuthentication();
  static const defaultEmail = 'admin@kumarfoods.local';
  static const _emailKey = 'kf_v3_admin_email';
  static const _saltKey = 'kf_v3_salt';
  static const _hashKey = 'kf_v3_password_digest';
  static const _bioKey = 'kf_v3_biometric_enabled';

  static Future<bool> get configured async => (await store.read(key:_hashKey))?.isNotEmpty == true;
  static Future<String> get email async => (await store.read(key:_emailKey)) ?? defaultEmail;
  static Future<bool> get biometricEnabled async => (await store.read(key:_bioKey)) == 'true';
  static String _newSalt() {
    final r=Random.secure();
    return base64UrlEncode(List<int>.generate(24, (_)=>r.nextInt(256)));
  }
  static String _digest(String salt, String password) {
    // Password verifier stored in Android Keystore-backed secure storage.
    // Iterations make offline guessing more expensive; never store raw password.
    List<int> output = utf8.encode('$salt:$password');
    for(var i=0;i<18000;i++) {output=sha256.convert(output).bytes;}
    return base64UrlEncode(output);
  }
  static bool _equals(String a,String b) {
    if(a.length!=b.length) return false;
    var diff=0;
    for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++) {diff |= a.codeUnitAt(i)^b.codeUnitAt(i);}
    return diff==0;
  }
  static Future<void> setup({required String email,required String password}) async {
    if(await configured) throw StateError('Already initialized. Use change credentials.');
    if(password.length<10 || email.trim().isEmpty) throw ArgumentError('Use an email and password of at least 10 characters');
    final salt=_newSalt();
    await store.write(key:_saltKey,value:salt);
    await store.write(key:_hashKey,value:_digest(salt,password));
    await store.write(key:_emailKey,value:email.trim().toLowerCase());
  }
  static Future<bool> verify(String email,String password) async {
    final oldEmail=await LocalGate.email;
    final salt=await store.read(key:_saltKey);
    final hash=await store.read(key:_hashKey);
    return salt!=null && hash!=null && email.trim().toLowerCase()==oldEmail && _equals(hash,_digest(salt,password));
  }
  static Future<void> change({required String currentPassword,required String newEmail,required String newPassword}) async {
    if(!await verify(await email,currentPassword)) throw StateError('Current password is incorrect');
    if(newEmail.trim().isEmpty || newPassword.length<10) throw ArgumentError('New password must be at least 10 characters');
    final salt=_newSalt();
    await store.write(key:_saltKey,value:salt);
    await store.write(key:_hashKey,value:_digest(salt,newPassword));
    await store.write(key:_emailKey,value:newEmail.trim().toLowerCase());
    await store.write(key:_bioKey,value:'false');
  }
  static Future<bool> canUseBiometric() async {
    try {return await biometric.canCheckBiometrics && (await biometric.getAvailableBiometrics()).isNotEmpty;}
    catch (_) {return false;}
  }
  static Future<void> enableBiometric(String password) async {
    if(!await verify(await email,password)) throw StateError('Password not accepted');
    if(!await canUseBiometric()) throw StateError('Fingerprint not enrolled in Android Settings');
    if(!await biometricUnlock()) throw StateError('Fingerprint verification was cancelled');
    await store.write(key:_bioKey,value:'true');
  }
  static Future<void> disableBiometric() async {await store.write(key:_bioKey,value:'false');}
  static Future<bool> biometricUnlock() async {
    try {
      if(!await biometricEnabled && !await configured) return false;
      if(!await canUseBiometric()) return false;
      return await biometric.authenticate(localizedReason:'Unlock Kumar Foods ERP Mobile',
        options:const AuthenticationOptions(biometricOnly:true,stickyAuth:true));
    } catch (_) {return false;}
  }
}

num money(dynamic value) => value is num ? value : num.tryParse('$value') ?? 0;

String dateOnly(dynamic v) {
  final date = DateTime.tryParse('${v ?? ''}');
  if (date == null) return '${v ?? ''}';
  return '${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${date.year}';
}
List<Map<String,dynamic>> records(Map<String,dynamic> snapshot, String key) {

  if (raw is! List) return [];
  return raw.whereType<Map>().map((row) => Map<String,dynamic>.from(row)).toList();

class MobileViewer extends StatefulWidget {

class _MobileViewerState extends State<MobileViewer> {
  static const vault = FlutterSecureStorage();
  // Public project identifier and public API key are not secret credentials.
  static const companyUrl = 'https://tuqhbqsxqjzahcqhascd.supabase.co';
  static const companyPublicKey = 'sb_publishable_sFC4Q5cqfTkJvqt4OFEwyg_5gTkuLJX';
  final pairCodeController = TextEditingController();
  final securityOldPassword=TextEditingController();
  final securityNewPassword=TextEditingController();
  final securityNewEmail=TextEditingController();
  String logoStyle = 'wheat_roti';
  bool nightHero = false;
  Timer? timer;

  @override void dispose() {timer?.cancel();pairCodeController.dispose();securityOldPassword.dispose();securityNewPassword.dispose();securityNewEmail.dispose();super.dispose();}
  Future<void> _initialize() async {
    try {
      _pairCode=await vault.read(key:'erp_mobile_pair_code_v2') ?? '';
      final saved=await vault.read(key:'erp_snapshot_v2') ?? '';
      if(saved.isNotEmpty) {
        final local=Map<String,dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(saved) as Map);
        final issues=WindowsMirrorData.audit(local);
        if(issues.isEmpty) {
          snapshot=local;
          lastSynced=DateTime.tryParse('${local['generatedAt']??''}');
          cloudUploadedAt=DateTime.tryParse('${local['_mobileCloudUpdatedAt']??''}');
          syncDriftWarning='${local['_syncDriftWarning']??''}';
        } else {
          snapshotIssues=issues;
          error='Offline data failed verification. Sync Now to get the office copy.';
        }
      }
      paired=_pairCode.length>=32;
    } catch (_) {error='Saved offline data could not be opened. Please Sync Now.';}
    try { logoStyle=await vault.read(key:'kf_logo_style_v1') ?? 'wheat_roti'; } catch (_) {}
    try { nightHero=(await vault.read(key:'kf_night_hero_v1'))=='true'; } catch (_) {}
    if(!mounted)return;
    setState((){});
    if(paired) await refresh();
    timer=Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds:30),(_){if(paired && !loading)refresh();});
  }
  // package:http selects browser fetch/XHR on Chrome and the native client on Android.
  // The pair code is private; never put it in a URL or analytics event.
  Future<(int,String)> _request(String code) async {
    final response=await http.post(
      Uri.parse('$companyUrl/rest/v1/rpc/erp_mobile_pull'),
      headers:{'apikey':companyPublicKey, 'Content-Type':'application/json',
        'Cache-Control':'no-store'},
      body:jsonEncode({'p_pair_code':code}),
    ).timeout(const Duration(seconds:35));
    return (response.statusCode,response.body);
  }
  /// Use platform-backed encrypted storage where it works. In a Chrome debug
  /// environment where secure storage is unavailable, permit memory-only
  /// pairing for the current session, never unencrypted browser storage.
  Future<void> _rememberPairing(String code) async {
    try {
      await vault.write(key:'erp_mobile_pair_code_v2',value:code);
      pairingSessionOnly=false;
    } catch (_) {
      pairingSessionOnly=true;
    }
    _pairCode=code;
  }

  Future<bool> _loadCloud(String code,{bool savePair=false}) async {
    if(loading)return false;
    setState(() {loading=true;error='';});
    try {
      final result=await _request(code);
      if(result.$1!=200) throw FormatException(result.$1==401||result.$1==403
          ? 'Pairing Code rejected. Check Windows ERP Settings.'
          : 'Cloud returned HTTP ${result.$1}. Check if Cloud Read-only SQL was deployed.');
      final raw=jsonDecode(result.$2);
      if(raw is! Map) throw const FormatException('Invalid cloud response');
      // The server can return HTTP 200 even for a rejected pairing code.
      // Never store an unverified code or delete a valid offline snapshot.
      if(raw['error']!=null) throw FormatException('${raw['error']}');
      final data=raw['payload'];
      if(raw['ready']==false || data==null){
        if(savePair) {
          final previousCode=_pairCode;
          await _rememberPairing(code);
          if (code != previousCode) {
            try {await vault.delete(key:'erp_snapshot_v2');} catch (_) {}
            snapshot=null;lastSynced=null;
          }
        }
        if(mounted)setState(() {paired=_pairCode.isNotEmpty;live=false;
          error='Cloud linked. No office data uploaded yet. Use Sync Now on Windows ERP.';});
        return true;
      }
      if(data is! Map || data['schema']!=1 || data['customers'] is! List || data['sales'] is! List){
        throw const FormatException('Unknown ERP snapshot format');
      }
      final parsed=Map<String,dynamic>.from(data);
      final issues=WindowsMirrorData.audit(parsed);
      if(issues.isNotEmpty) {
        throw FormatException('Office snapshot did not pass accounting checks: ${issues.join(' ')} Previous verified data is unchanged.');
      }
      final date=DateTime.tryParse('${parsed['generatedAt']??''}');
      final uploaded=DateTime.tryParse('${raw['updatedAt'] ?? ''}');
      if(date==null || uploaded==null) {
        throw const FormatException('Office snapshot has no valid upload timestamp.');
      }
      // Use the database's server upload time, not the Windows machine clock.
      // Developer PC and office PC may have different clock settings.
      if((!savePair || code == _pairCode) && cloudUploadedAt != null &&
          uploaded.isBefore(cloudUploadedAt!)) {
        throw const FormatException('Cloud returned an older uploaded version. The last verified data is preserved.');
      }
      final age=DateTime.now().toUtc().difference(uploaded.toUtc()).inSeconds;
      // Catch a second / outdated PC overwriting a newer office snapshot.
      // Legitimate deletions may also lower the count; warn, do not rewrite data.
      final seenSales=WindowsMirrorData.sales(snapshot??<String,dynamic>{})
          .map((v)=>'${v['number']??''}'.trim()).where((v)=>v.isNotEmpty).toSet();
      final receivedSales=WindowsMirrorData.sales(parsed)
          .map((v)=>'${v['number']??''}'.trim()).where((v)=>v.isNotEmpty).toSet();
      final missingVouchers=seenSales.difference(receivedSales);
      final driftMessage=missingVouchers.isEmpty ? '' :
          'WARNING: ${missingVouchers.length} previously seen Sale vouchers are absent in the current cloud upload. '
          'Verify which Windows PC uploaded it and reconcile before treating totals as final.';
      // Timestamp is mobile-only metadata, never a new financial voucher.
      final localCopy=<String,dynamic>{...parsed,'_mobileCloudUpdatedAt':raw['updatedAt'],
        '_syncDriftWarning':driftMessage};
      if(savePair) {
        await _rememberPairing(code);
      }
      // A browser may have a small storage quota. Keep the live Windows data
      // usable even if storing all invoice PDFs offline exceeds that quota.
      var localCacheWarning = '';
      try {
        await vault.write(key:'erp_snapshot_v2',value:jsonEncode(localCopy));
      } catch (_) {
        // NEVER throw away current financial data to make room for PDF bytes.
        // A limited offline cache must be labelled explicitly, not silently.
        localCacheWarning = 'Invoice PDFs are available only while online: device storage limit reached.';
        final light=<String,dynamic>{...localCopy};
        for (final key in const ['salesRegister','standardSales','sales']) {
          if (parsed[key] is List) {
            light[key]=records(parsed,key).map((row)=>
              <String,dynamic>{...row}..remove('invoicePdfBase64')).toList();
          }
        }
        try { await vault.write(key:'erp_snapshot_v2',value:jsonEncode(light)); }
        catch (_) { localCacheWarning='Offline storage failed. Data is visible now but may not remain after reopening the app.'; }
      }
      if(!mounted)return true;
      setState((){
        paired=true;snapshot=parsed;lastSynced=date;cloudUploadedAt=uploaded;
        syncDriftWarning=driftMessage;
        snapshotIssues=<String>[];
        cacheWarning=localCacheWarning;
        live=age>=-120 && age<=150;
        error=age>150?'Latest cloud upload is old. Check Sync Now on OFFICE Windows ERP.':'';
      });
      return true;
    } catch(e){if(mounted)setState(() {live=false;error='Cloud sync: $e';});return false;}
    finally {if(mounted)setState(()=>loading=false);}
  }
  Future<void> connect() async {
    final code=pairCodeController.text.trim();
    if(code.length<32){setState(()=>error='Enter the 32+ character Mobile Pairing Code from Windows ERP Settings.');return;}
    final ok=await _loadCloud(code,savePair:true);
    if(ok){pairCodeController.clear();if(mounted)openTab('Home');}
  }
  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if(_pairCode.isEmpty){if(mounted)setState(()=>error='Connect once in Settings to view office cloud data.');return;}
    await _loadCloud(_pairCode);
  }
  Future<void> forgetPairing() async {
    try {await vault.delete(key:'erp_mobile_pair_code_v2');} catch (_) {}
    try {await vault.delete(key:'erp_snapshot_v2');} catch (_) {}
    if(!mounted)return;
    setState(() {paired=false;live=false;snapshot=null;lastSynced=null;cloudUploadedAt=null;
      snapshotIssues=<String>[];cacheWarning='';error='';tab='Home';_pairCode='';pairingSessionOnly=false;pairCodeController.clear();});
  }

  void openTab(String target,{int? id,String name='',bool keepDates=false}) {
    setState((){
      if(target=='Account Ledger' && tab!='Account Ledger') _parentTab=tab;
      tab=target;accountId=id;accountName=name;query='';if(!keepDates){fromDate=null;toDate=null;}
    });
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final data=snapshot ?? <String,dynamic>{};
    return PopScope(canPop:false,onPopInvokedWithResult:(didPop,result){
      if(!didPop) _handleSystemBack();
    },child:Scaffold(
      appBar:tab=='Home'?null:AppBar(
        leading:IconButton(icon:const Icon(Icons.arrow_back,size:28),
          onPressed:()=>openTab(tab=='Account Ledger'?_parentTab:(tab=='Connection'||tab=='Security & Login'?'Settings':'Home'),keepDates:tab=='Account Ledger')),
        title:Text(tab=='Account Ledger'?'Ledger':(tab=='Payment & Receipt'?'Receipt List':tab)),
        actions:[if(tab=='Account Ledger')IconButton(tooltip:'Print / Share PDF',
          icon:const Icon(Icons.ios_share_outlined,color:ink),onPressed:_printCurrentLedger)
          else IconButton(tooltip:'Sync Now',icon:const Icon(Icons.cloud_sync_outlined),onPressed:loading?null:refresh)],
      ),
      body:Column(children:[
        if(syncDriftWarning.isNotEmpty) MaterialBanner(
          backgroundColor:const Color(0xFFFFE3DF),
          content:Text(syncDriftWarning,style:const TextStyle(fontSize:12,
            color:Color(0xFF8D242A),fontWeight:FontWeight.w700)),
          actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>openTab('Connection'),
            child:const Text('VERIFY SYNC'))],
        ),
        if(!paired || !live) MaterialBanner(
          backgroundColor:const Color(0xFFFFF7E3),
          content:Text(!paired?'Connect your Windows ERP in Settings to see cloud data.'
              : (error.isEmpty?'OFFLINE · Showing last saved cloud data':error),
            style:const TextStyle(fontSize:12,color:ink)),
          actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>openTab('Settings'),child:const Text('SETTINGS'))]),
        Expanded(child:Center(child:ConstrainedBox(
          constraints:const BoxConstraints(maxWidth:850),
          child:tab=='Home'?_home(data):_listing(data)))),
      ]),
    ));
  }

  String _syncLabel(BuildContext context) {
    if(lastSynced==null)return 'Not connected';
    final d=lastSynced!.toLocal();
    return '${dateOnly(d.toIso8601String())} ${TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(d).format(context)}';
  }
